[2025-04-08T21:48:24.336Z] [SQL] Query: getWarehouseStatistics
DATA: {
  "sql": "\n      WITH inventory_stats AS (\n        SELECT \n          COUNT(DISTINCT i.product_id) as product_count,\n          COUNT(CASE WHEN i.quantity <= COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 0) AND i.product_id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as critical_item_count,\n          COALESCE(SUM(i.quantity * COALESCE(p.price, 0)), 0) as inventory_value\n        FROM \n          inventory_items i\n        LEFT JOIN\n          products p ON i.product_id = p.id\n        WHERE \n          i.warehouse_id = $1\n      ),\n      machine_count AS (\n        SELECT \n          COUNT(DISTINCT machine_id) as machine_count\n        FROM \n          machine_warehouse_assignments\n        WHERE \n          warehouse_id = $1 AND is_active = true\n      )\n      SELECT \n        COALESCE(i.product_count, 0) as product_count,\n        COALESCE(i.critical_item_count, 0) as critical_item_count,\n        COALESCE(i.inventory_value, 0) as inventory_value,\n        COALESCE(m.machine_count, 0) as machine_count\n      FROM \n        (SELECT 1) dummy\n      LEFT JOIN inventory_stats i ON true\n      LEFT JOIN machine_count m ON true\n    ",
  "params": [
    4
  ]

[2025-04-08T21:48:24.337Z] [SQL] Query: getWarehouseInventory
DATA: {
  "sql": "\n      SELECT \n        i.id,\n        i.warehouse_id,\n        i.product_id,\n        p.product_name,\n        i.quantity,\n        i.min_quantity,\n        p.price,\n        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM product_batches pb \n          WHERE pb.product_id = i.product_id AND pb.warehouse_id = i.warehouse_id) as batch_count,\n        (SELECT MAX(im.performed_at) FROM inventory_movements im \n          WHERE im.product_id = i.product_id AND \n            ((im.source_type = 'warehouse' AND im.source_id = i.warehouse_id) OR \n            (im.destination_type = 'warehouse' AND im.destination_id = i.warehouse_id))) as last_movement_date,\n        CASE WHEN i.quantity <= COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 0) THEN true ELSE false END as is_critical\n      FROM \n        inventory_items i\n      JOIN \n        products p ON i.product_id = p.id\n      WHERE \n        i.warehouse_id = $1\n      ORDER BY \n        p.product_name ASC\n    ",

  "sql": "\n      SELECT \n        i.id,\n        i.warehouse_id,\n        i.product_id,\n        p.product_name,\n        i.quantity,\n        i.min_quantity,\n        p.price,\n        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM product_batches pb \n          WHERE pb.product_id = i.product_id AND pb.warehouse_id = i.warehouse_id) as batch_count,\n        (SELECT MAX(im.performed_at) FROM inventory_movements im \n          WHERE im.product_id = i.product_id AND \n            (im.source_warehouse_id = i.warehouse_id OR \n             im.destination_warehouse_id = i.warehouse_id)) as last_movement_date,\n        CASE WHEN i.quantity <= COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 0) THEN true ELSE false END as is_critical\n      FROM \n        inventory_items i\n      JOIN \n        products p ON i.product_id = p.id\n      WHERE \n        i.warehouse_id = $1\n      ORDER BY \n        p.product_name ASC\n    ",

[2025-04-09T09:21:37.418Z] [WarehouseStats] Fetching warehouse statistics for ID: 6

[2025-04-09T09:21:37.418Z] [SQL] Query: getWarehouseStatistics
DATA: {
  "sql": "\n      WITH inventory_stats AS (\n        SELECT \n          COUNT(DISTINCT i.product_id) as product_count,\n          COUNT(CASE WHEN i.quantity <= COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 0) AND i.product_id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as critical_item_count,\n          COALESCE(SUM(i.quantity * COALESCE(p.price, 0)), 0) as inventory_value\n        FROM \n          inventory_items i\n        LEFT JOIN\n          products p ON i.product_id = p.id\n        WHERE \n          i.warehouse_id = $1\n      ),\n      machine_count AS (\n        SELECT \n          COUNT(DISTINCT machine_id) as machine_count\n        FROM \n          machine_warehouse_assignments\n        WHERE \n          warehouse_id = $1\n      )\n      SELECT \n        COALESCE(i.product_count, 0) as product_count,\n        COALESCE(i.critical_item_count, 0) as critical_item_count,\n        COALESCE(i.inventory_value, 0) as inventory_value,\n        COALESCE(m.machine_count, 0) as machine_count\n      FROM \n        (SELECT 1) dummy\n      LEFT JOIN inventory_stats i ON true\n      LEFT JOIN machine_count m ON true\n    ",
  "params": [
    6
  ]
}

DATA: {
  "sql": "\n      SELECT \n        i.id,\n        i.warehouse_id,\n        i.product_id,\n        p.product_name,\n        i.quantity,\n        i.min_quantity,\n        p.price,\n        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM product_batches pb \n          WHERE pb.product_id = i.product_id AND pb.warehouse_id = i.warehouse_id) as batch_count,\n        (SELECT MAX(im.performed_at) FROM inventory_movements im \n          WHERE im.product_id = i.product_id AND \n            (im.source_warehouse_id = i.warehouse_id OR \n             im.destination_warehouse_id = i.warehouse_id)) as last_movement_date,\n        CASE WHEN i.quantity <= COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 0) THEN true ELSE false END as is_critical\n      FROM \n        inventory_items i\n      JOIN \n        products p ON i.product_id = p.id\n      WHERE \n        i.warehouse_id = $1\n      ORDER BY \n        p.product_name ASC\n    ",

[2025-04-09T14:47:37.841Z] [WarehouseStatsAPI] Fetching statistics for warehouse 4 using new service

[2025-04-09T14:47:37.841Z] [WarehouseStats] Fetching warehouse statistics for ID: 4

[2025-04-09T14:47:37.842Z] [SQL] Query: getWarehouseStatistics
DATA: {
  "sql": "\n      WITH inventory_stats AS (\n        SELECT \n          COUNT(DISTINCT i.product_id) as product_count,\n          COUNT(CASE WHEN i.quantity <= COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 0) AND i.product_id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as critical_item_count,\n          COALESCE(SUM(i.quantity * COALESCE(p.price, 0)), 0) as inventory_value\n        FROM \n          inventory_items i\n        LEFT JOIN\n          products p ON i.product_id = p.id\n        WHERE \n          i.warehouse_id = $1\n      ),\n      machine_count AS (\n        SELECT \n          COUNT(DISTINCT machine_id) as machine_count\n        FROM \n          machine_warehouse_assignments\n        WHERE \n          warehouse_id = $1\n      )\n      SELECT \n        COALESCE(i.product_count, 0) as product_count,\n        COALESCE(i.critical_item_count, 0) as critical_item_count,\n        COALESCE(i.inventory_value, 0) as inventory_value,\n        COALESCE(m.machine_count, 0) as machine_count\n      FROM \n        (SELECT 1) dummy\n      LEFT JOIN inventory_stats i ON true\n      LEFT JOIN machine_count m ON true\n    ",
  "params": [
    4
  ]
}

DATA: {
  "sql": "\n      SELECT \n        i.id,\n        i.warehouse_id,\n        i.product_id,\n        p.product_name,\n        i.quantity,\n        i.min_quantity,\n        p.price,\n        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM product_batches pb \n          WHERE pb.product_id = i.product_id AND pb.warehouse_id = i.warehouse_id) as batch_count,\n        (SELECT MAX(im.performed_at) FROM inventory_movements im \n          WHERE im.product_id = i.product_id AND \n            (im.source_warehouse_id = i.warehouse_id OR \n             im.destination_warehouse_id = i.warehouse_id)) as last_movement_date,\n        CASE WHEN i.quantity <= COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 0) THEN true ELSE false END as is_critical\n      FROM \n        inventory_items i\n      JOIN \n        products p ON i.product_id = p.id\n      WHERE \n        i.warehouse_id = $1\n      ORDER BY \n        p.product_name ASC\n    ",
  "params": [
    4

DATA: {

DATA: {
  "sql": "\n      SELECT \n        i.id,\n        $1 as warehouse_id,\n        p.id as product_id,\n        p.product_name,\n        COALESCE(i.quantity, 0) as quantity,\n        COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 5) as min_quantity,\n        p.price,\n        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM product_batches pb \n          WHERE pb.product_id = p.id AND pb.warehouse_id = $1) as batch_count,\n        (SELECT MAX(im.performed_at) FROM inventory_movements im \n          WHERE im.product_id = p.id AND \n            (im.source_warehouse_id = $1 OR \n             im.destination_warehouse_id = $1)) as last_movement_date,\n        CASE WHEN COALESCE(i.quantity, 0) <= COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 5) THEN true ELSE false END as is_critical\n      FROM \n        products p\n      LEFT JOIN \n        inventory_items i ON i.product_id = p.id AND i.warehouse_id = $1\n      ORDER BY \n        p.product_name ASC\n    ",
  "params": [

DATA: {
  "sql": "\n      SELECT \n        i.id,\n        $1 as warehouse_id,\n        p.id as product_id,\n        p.product_name,\n        COALESCE(i.quantity, 0) as quantity,\n        COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 5) as min_quantity,\n        p.price,\n        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM product_batches pb \n          WHERE pb.product_id = p.id AND pb.warehouse_id = $1) as batch_count,\n        (SELECT MAX(im.performed_at) FROM inventory_movements im \n          WHERE im.product_id = p.id AND \n            (im.source_warehouse_id = $1 OR \n             im.destination_warehouse_id = $1)) as last_movement_date,\n        CASE WHEN COALESCE(i.quantity, 0) <= COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 5) THEN true ELSE false END as is_critical\n      FROM \n        products p\n      LEFT JOIN \n        inventory_items i ON i.product_id = p.id AND i.warehouse_id = $1\n      ORDER BY \n        p.product_name ASC\n    ",
  "params": [

[2025-04-15T12:15:38.877Z] [WarehouseStats] Fetching warehouse statistics for ID: 4

[2025-04-15T12:15:38.877Z] [SQL] Query: getWarehouseStatistics
DATA: {
  "sql": "\n      WITH inventory_stats AS (\n        SELECT \n          (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM products) as product_count,\n          COUNT(CASE WHEN i.quantity <= COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 0) AND i.product_id IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as critical_item_count,\n          COALESCE(SUM(i.quantity * COALESCE(p.price, 0)), 0) as inventory_value\n        FROM \n          inventory_items i\n        LEFT JOIN\n          products p ON i.product_id = p.id\n        WHERE \n          i.warehouse_id = $1\n      ),\n      machine_count AS (\n        SELECT \n          COUNT(DISTINCT machine_id) as machine_count\n        FROM \n          machine_warehouse_assignments\n        WHERE \n          warehouse_id = $1\n      )\n      SELECT \n        COALESCE(i.product_count, 0) as product_count,\n        COALESCE(i.critical_item_count, 0) as critical_item_count,\n        COALESCE(i.inventory_value, 0) as inventory_value,\n        COALESCE(m.machine_count, 0) as machine_count\n      FROM \n        (SELECT 1) dummy\n      LEFT JOIN inventory_stats i ON true\n      LEFT JOIN machine_count m ON true\n    ",
  "params": [
    4
  ]
}

[2025-04-15T12:15:38.881Z] [InventoryAPI] Bestandsabfrage für Lager ID 4 mit neuem Service

[2025-04-15T12:15:38.881Z] [SQL] Query: getWarehouseInventory
DATA: {
  "sql": "\n      SELECT \n        i.id,\n        $1 as warehouse_id,\n        p.id as product_id,\n        p.product_name,\n        COALESCE(i.quantity, 0) as quantity,\n        COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 5) as min_quantity,\n        p.price,\n        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM product_batches pb \n          WHERE pb.product_id = p.id AND pb.warehouse_id = $1) as batch_count,\n        (SELECT MAX(im.performed_at) FROM inventory_movements im \n          WHERE im.product_id = p.id AND \n            (im.source_warehouse_id = $1 OR \n             im.destination_warehouse_id = $1)) as last_movement_date,\n        CASE WHEN COALESCE(i.quantity, 0) <= COALESCE(i.min_quantity, 5) THEN true ELSE false END as is_critical\n      FROM \n        products p\n      LEFT JOIN \n        inventory_items i ON i.product_id = p.id AND i.warehouse_id = $1\n      ORDER BY \n        p.product_name ASC\n    ",
